SDD V2.59 2026-09-25 Kimi 收听专用界面（listen-only 角色 + 独立收听密码）。 需求：给访客开一个「只能听」的入口 —— 可以调整频率和模式、听音频、看频谱，但绝不能发射、不能改任何设备设置，且密码与操作员密码分开。新增 MRRC_LISTEN_PASSWORD（默认空 = 不启用收听登录）：用它登录得到带 listen 角色的 token（_auth_tokens 保持不变，_listen_tokens 标记角色），登录接口返回 role，登录页对 listen 角色跳转到新页面 /listen（static/listen.html + 自包含 static/listen.js：大频率显示、直接输频/频段快捷键、模式按钮、记忆频道调用（memRecall 只写频率+模式）、S 表、频谱瀑布、RX 音频 + 浏览器端音量；复用 rx_worklet_processor.js 与 WASM Opus 解码，不加载两个受 tooling-guard 保护的 monolith JS）。管控全部在服务端（UI 隐藏从来不是执行点）：_handle_ws_message 角色门只放行 ping/get/memLoadAll/memRecall 与 set{freq,vfo_a_freq,vfo_b_freq,mode}，其余（含 legacy 冒号格式、memSave/memDelete、recording/cq/ptt/tune/全部设备设置）回 LISTEN_ONLY_MESSAGE 错误；/WSaudioTX 与 /WSatr1000 对 listen token 直接 close 4003（拿不到麦克上行与天调继电器命令）；auth 中间件对 listen 角色的非 GET /api/*（logout 除外）回 403，REST 只读。口令比对走 hmac.compare_digest（I9 同款棘轮测试钉住）；两个密码相同时按 admin 处理（操作员设成一样时期望全控而非受限）。测试 1330→1356（+26：口令匹配 3、登录角色 7、WS 门 7、端点门 5、代理契约 4）；sdd_context check 无新增告警，全套绿。公网映射（同日，应操作员要求）：https://www.vlsc.net/mrrc_modern/listen 经 www 主机 nginx 直连反向代理到电台机（无 SSH 隧道）。后端先用 IPv6 字面量上线，当晚切换为 DNS 名 radio.vlsc.net:8888（AAAA）——操作员新建 AAAA 记录时旧字面量的前缀已经变了（字面量方案当场失效的活例证）；nginx 侧用 proxy_pass 变量 + 每个 location 的 resolver 1.1.1.1 8.8.8.8 valid=300s，家中 IPv6 再变最多 300 s 自动重解析，无需 reload。listen 页面改为前缀感知（URL_BASE 从 location.pathname 推导，直连 /listen 与代理 /mrrc_modern/listen 同一份代码；login 内联 JS 与静态资源全部相对路径），新增 deploy_listen_proxy.sh（幂等 marker 插入，模式同 deploy_support_receiver.sh）。实施期两个真教训：① www 站点既有正则 location（静态资源 \\.(css | js | …)$ 本地规则）会盖过普通前缀匹配，把 /mrrc_modern/modules/*.js 截成本地 404 —— 三个前缀 location 必须 ^~（该配置文件自己的注释也写着这个惯例）；② 提取旧块的正则没匹配^~形式导致重复插入、nginx -t直接挂掉 —— 脚本改为删除全部旧块再插入唯一新块，并有结构测试钉住。边界：经代理的登录限流按来源 IP 聚合（电台机看到的是 www 主机地址）；收听密码由操作员自管（.env MRRC_LISTEN_PASSWORD）。
iOS 收听（同日晚补）：iPhone Safari 首次进页有点击「▶ 开始收听」手势激活 AudioContext（自动播放策略），并补上与主 UI 同款的 ScriptProcessor 回退（AudioWorklet 在 iOS 上不可用时 60 ms 预缓冲 / 300 ms 上限，此前回退缺失会导致苹果浏览器完全无声）。iOS 三连坑（现场逐一定位）：① Web Audio 挂在 ambient 音频会话上整体哑掉 —— 操作员现场报告「主界面按 PTT 后才有声」，根因即 getUserMedia 把会话切到 PlayAndRecord 的副作用；收听页照此在启动时请求并立即释放麦克风（mic✓ 诊断可见，不录音）；② 首版把激活 await 在音频链第一步，而弹窗在内嵌 webview 永不落定 → 音频 WS 永不连接（日志里控制/频谱在、WSaudioRX 缺席），改并行 + 10 s 超时；③ 锁屏断音 —— 移植 legacy mrrc mobile_modern.js的 Screen Wake Lock（手势申请 + visibilitychange 重申请，诊断行 🔒）。页面加底部诊断行（ctx 状态/帧计数/解码电平 lvl/缓冲 ms）与「测试声音」按钮（<audio> 会话激活 + 同链路 880Hz 测试音），把「没声音」一刀切成输出通路/流内容/解码三段。一屏紧凑布局（100dvh、瀑布固定 clamp(90px,16dvh,150px) 高度、波段/模式/记忆芯片横排，参照收发主界面）；记忆频道 3×2 网格全显；页脚在线人数 —— 服务端新增 onlineUsers 广播（/WSradio 每次连接/断开推送 len(ctrl_clients)，其它 UI 忽略未知类型）；收听角色频谱降帧（_spectrum_frame_due每 3 帧发 1 帧，30 Hz→~10 Hz，省约 2/3 频谱流量，操作员要求）；页脚网络指示（三通道接收字节速率 ↓KB/s + /WSradio ping/pong RTT ms，每秒刷新，操作员要求）；公网抖动缓冲（收听页 RX jitter buffer 由主界面 LAN 取向的 120/300 ms 加大到 500/250/1500 ms，ScriptProcessor 回退 400 ms/1.5 s —— 公网链路 RTT/抖动远超局域网，紧凑缓冲表现为卡顿，收听场景不在意亚秒级延迟，操作员现场反馈）；频率步进（移植主界面的预设步进模式：◀/▶ 单步、◀◀/▶▶ 五倍，步进钮循环 10Hz–25kHz，30kHz–75MHz 边界内）；FFT 迹线（瀑布上方 42% 区域新增 #fft-canvas，简化版 renderFFTPlot：网格 + EMA(0.3) 平滑 + 琥珀迹线与渐变填充，无频率刻度——收听角色本就不能改 span）。listen.js?v=13。测试 1330→1361（+31：口令 3、登录 7、WS 门 7、端点门 8、代理/页面契约 6）。文档：§9.2 角色说明 + onlineUsers + 降帧、AGENTS.md、README env 表、tests/README、CHANGELOG。

SDD V2.57 2026-09-23 Claude ATR-1000 驻波超阈值自动完整调谐 + QRP 学习门限（对齐兄弟项目 mrrc V5.8.0/V5.8.5）。 现场需求：大驻波天线上发射时天调不会自己动手，操作员必须停下手动按 TUNE；学习库也只在服务端知道 TX 时才更新。新增高驻波守卫（atr1000_client.py，实例状态、无模块全局、无锁）：操作者已在发射（实测功率 ≥5 W）且 SWR 严格 >2.0 持续 ≥1.5 s → 向天调发一帧完整调谐 FF 04 01 02（mode=2），30 s 冷却；同一频点连续 3 次无改善则 auto_giveup 放弃，直到频率变化或 SWR 回落；频率变化 >1 kHz 或继电器刚变（1 s 忽略窗）重启连续段；调谐期间（含手动 assist 的 TX2 载波）由既有 _tuning 标志互斥，不重复触发。绝不自行键控电台：守卫只发天调帧，模块内不存在 CAT/PTT 引用（源码护栏测试固化）——触发前提就是操作者自己的发射。调谐后写回：每条清调谐路径（relay stable >5 s / same-relay confirm >1.5 s / TX 结束 / 45 s 超时 / 设备显式 TUNE_STATUS=0）首次挂接 0.8 s 后的比对，仅当 SWR 改善 ≥0.02 且终值 ≤1.8 才 learn(force_update=True) 写回 atr1000_tuner.json；不回滚继电器（语音 QSO 中途回滚到已知差的匹配比调谐结果更糟）；连接断开或帧发送失败发 auto_aborted 终态事件（否则前端进行态会永久卡在 ···）。学习门限：入口由「服务端 TX 信号」改为「实测功率 ≥3 W」（QRP 友好；idle 漏读约 1–2 W 不会误学），因此面板直发 PTT / 外部软件发射也会学习（兄弟项目 V5.8.5 根因 2 的同款修复）。前端：复用既有 atrTuneResult 通道（auto=true）+ 6 个 auto 阶段 toast（触发/成功/无改善/超时/已放弃/中断），进行期间 TUNE 按钮显示 ··· 并禁点；sw.js 缓存版本 atr1000.js?v=2。服务端：_on_atr_tune_event 转发到 /WSatr1000；手动 _start_atr_tune_assist() 增加 read_state()["tuning"] 前置检查（关掉轮询 tx_status ≤500 ms 滞后的竞态窗口）。实现期由测试抓到的两个真缺陷：① 守卫原判定 if self._swr_high_freq and abs(...) 在首次见到频率时（_swr_high_freq == 0）不重置连续段，旧连续段会跨 QSY 触发；② LearningBufferTests 把旧门限写死为字面量 4.9/5.0 W，门限降到 3 W 后会静默失效（已改为从 LEARN_MIN_POWER 派生）。套件 1299→1330（+31：守卫 13、比对与 worker 9、学习门限净增 2、服务端 5、前端护栏 2）；AGENTS.md／tests/README.md／CHANGELOG.md 同步。边界：真机（FT-710 + ATR-1000 + 大驻波天线）端到端验收待现场执行 —— 重点是发射中被吃掉的一小段体感是否可接受、以及写回后二次访问该频点是否省掉整次调谐；_retune_fail_count 只活在进程内，重启即清零（刻意不持久化）。
SDD V2.56 2026-09-19 Claude FDE 看板：支持回路的自我迭代成为常设实践（loop practice 4）。 需求：支持回路只有「分析→答复」，分类出的 bug/feature 没有去处，人每次都要手动挑、手动改、手动收尾。新增 support_board.py（数据模型/渲染/护栏，stdlib-only）与 dev_tools/fde_board.py（排期决策 CLI）：pi 分析契约加可选 kind(bug/feature/noise)+severity+title 字段（向后兼容，旧输出无字段则按 needs_code_change 回退 bug、否则 noise），每条分析自动入板；分类即决策：noise → 已答复闭环（答复页已回、无代码工作）、bug → 自动排期进后台实施队列、feature → 待决策（操作员 fde_board.py decide 排期/拒绝）；未真正答复（need_more_info）的条目不允许闭环，留在待决策跟进；cron 新增 --implement：对已排期条目单飞实施——pi 只读定位后以 unified-diff JSON 契约返回补丁（模型无写权限），护栏拒绝（工具护栏 JS/certs/packaging/>8 文件）→ git apply --check → 仅在独立 fde/<编号>分支应用，全套 unittest 绿才 commit，红则回滚分支；main 永不被无人值守进程触碰；no_action 判定自动进 Rejected。看板页 <www.vlsc.net/mrrc_modern/board/> 与答复页互链，同 redact_public 隐私过滤；cron 行升级为 --once --publish --implement（幂等重装）。事故修复：cron 里 pi 连续两天 SIGABRT（Homebrew 升级 llhttp 打断 bottled node 的 dylib 链接）——run_pi 信号死亡时日志点名成因与修复；测试隔离补 BOARD_STATE_FILE/BOARD_PAGE_PATH（fixture 曾污染真实看板）。套件 1276→1297（+21 看板测试）；release_check 0 failing。边界：fde/ 分支的人工合入与真实 bug 端到端（上报→排期→自动实施→合入）待首次真实排期；实施 pi 的探查预算与 diff 质量待真实样本校准。
SDD V2.55 2026-09-18 Claude PTT 释放→RX 声音恢复延迟优化（现场体感「滞后太长」，实测日志分解根因）。 现场日志证据：每次 restart_rx（reopening capture stream → RX audio started）稳定 262–300 ms（9 样本无一例外），加上 stop_tx 排空 ~70–130 ms、unkey 写可能排在轮询事务后（POLL_TIMEOUT 0.25 s）、以及 TX 期间服务端照发的静音 Opus 帧在前端 jitter buffer 里积压 ~120 ms（unkey 后必须先排空才听到真声音），合计 ~470–870 ms。三项修复（独立提交）：① skip_next_poll("tx_status") 提前到 release drain+unkey 之前（poll-stale-guard 本就要求 skip 先于 CAT 写；顺带消除 unkey 后 in-flight 轮询回读 stale PTT=1 翻回 UI 的竞态）；② _audio_rx_loop 在 radio.is_transmitting 时跳过 encode+fan-out（读取/录音 tap/静音 watchdog 保留 —— 半双工电台 TX 期间 USB RX 输出是位精确静音，广播它只会在每个客户端 jitter buffer 里堆静音），TX 结束后客户端处于 recovery gate（60 ms）待命，新帧一到立即起播，顺带省 TX 期间编码 CPU；③ start_rx 记录上次成功 open 的 (index, name) hint，TX→RX 重开时跳过 _find_rx_device 全设备列表扫描（≤4 层枚举，是 ~270 ms 的大头），hint 失效（USB 重枚举改名/换索引）自动回退全扫描；重开 settle 50→20 ms（stop_tx 已先睡 20 ms）。保留不动：AD-011 V2.14 的 capture reopen workaround 本身、stop_tx 的语音尾部 drain（≤50 ms + Pa_StopStream 排空）、ptt-release-no-verify（不加 verify 循环）。测试 1271→1276（+5：TX 静音不广播 1、设备 hint 3、settle 1）。边界：端到端延迟改善需真机验收 —— 用日志 TX session: → RX audio started 间隔与浏览器 window.__jitterMs 复核，预期 ~500–700 ms → ~250–350 ms；「TX 期间预重开捕获流」（把 270 ms 藏进发射时间）因 V2.33 流退化风险（playback 打开时 capture 重开可能立即再退化）未实施，如需要可做成 env 开关实验。文档：SDD/14 版本历史、AGENTS.md/tests-README 测试计数。
SDD V2.54 2026-09-18 Claude v1.18.1 补丁发布：macOS 装机两处致命修复 —— 签名（「已损坏」）与音频输入权限（RX 无声）。 两处都只在安装后暴露，且都不是电台/配置问题。①签名从未成功：Contents/_CodeSignature 始终缺失，codesign --verify 报 code has no resources but signature indicates they must be present，Gatekeeper 给出「已损坏，无法打开」（右键也绕不过去）；根因是 codesign 把 Contents/MacOS、Contents/Frameworks 下的数据文件/目录当嵌套代码而拒绝签整个 bundle，而 build.sh 把这次失败写成警告（non-fatal）—— v1.17.0 的 DMG 实测同样坏，即该缺陷自首次发版起一直存在。修法：数据树放 Contents/Resources/（资源位置被封存而非被当代码检查），只保留 Frameworks -> Resources 与 MacOS/_internal -> ../Resources 两条符号链接；MacOS 内除可执行文件外不得有数据（mem_channels.json/version.txt/macos//vendor/ 已移入资源树，启动器新增 runtime_path() 先 app_dir() 再回退 _internal/）；签名或校验失败即 exit 1，spctl 报 damaged 也中止（ad-hoc 下 rejected（无 Developer ID）是正常结果）。②音频输入权限缺失 → RX 静音：打包 Info.plist 无任何权限说明键，系统日志 tccd: Refusing authorization request for service kTCCServiceMicrophone … without NSMicrophoneUsageDescription key；macOS 把音频输入（电台 USB CODEC）归入麦克风权限类，缺键时 CoreAudio 照常让捕获流打开成功、却把缓冲填零 —— 日志只有 peak=0.0% 与误导性的”检查电台 AF 增益”。修法：Info.plist 加 NSMicrophoneUsageDescription（构建期断言缺键即中止），近静默警告在 frozen macOS 上改写系统设置路径。实测证据：修复包 codesign --verify = valid on disk + satisfies its Designated Requirement、_CodeSignature/CodeResources 存在、spctl 只报无 Developer ID、DMG 内外双重校验（内部应用 CFBundleShortVersionString=v1.18.1 + 麦克风键 + version.txt=1.18.1）、冻结服务 /api/update/check 自报 current=1.18.0（v1.18.0 包，同时证明更新通道与线上 latest.json 首次端到端打通）、替换安装后 tccd 由 Refusing … 变为 AUTHREQ_PROMPTING。产物：macOS MRRC-Modern-v1.18.1-arm64.dmg 55,643,537 bytes 931b312c…；Windows MRRC-Modern-v1.18.1-Windows-x64-Setup.exe 46,002,543 bytes 19b5cef2…。套件 1267→1271 项（含 10 项新增：启动器资源回退 6、macOS 权限提示 4）全绿；release_check.py 离线 29 ok / 0 failing。顺带修掉 harness 自身的编码缺陷：Windows VM 的 GBK 控制台下 ✓/·/✗ 让 reporter 抛 UnicodeEncodeError（把”规则违规”伪装成 traceback），改为 errors=backslashreplace。边界：麦克风授权需用户首次点「允许」，ad-hoc 签名下授权绑定当次构建 cdhash、今后每次升级都会重新询问（要免除需 Developer ID 签名）；Windows 包在 KVM 上构建（无物理声卡，TX 话音仍待真机验收）。、今后每次升级都会重新询问（要免除需 Developer ID 签名）；Windows 包在 KVM 上构建（无物理声卡，TX 话音仍待真机验收）。 发布后文档全面同步（同日）：docs/MACOS_INSTALLER_GUIDE.md 由 58 行扩写为完整使用指南（下载校验 → 安装 → 两种 Gatekeeper 弹窗的分辨 → 麦克风权限及其「静默失败」症状 → SDD V2.46 2026-09-12 Claude Yaesu 机型扩展：FTDX10 / FTDX101D / FTDX101MP / FTX-1F（实验性，仅接收）。 新增 profile 驱动的共享 Yaesu ASCII-CAT 核心 backends/yaesu/（AD-018）：yaesu_profiles.py（每机型差异的唯一来源 —— 模式寄存器与独立的 CAT 字符表、滤波槽位、频段、衰减/前置、功率格式、S 表曲线、溯源），cat_core.py（移植 FT-710 已验证的传输层：; 帧/AI 前缀过滤/写-only set/PTT 优先级抢占/ENXIO 分类/重连，按 profile 参数化，并含 Hamlib ftx1.c:881 的 VM000; 离开 memory 步骤），backend.py（由 profile 派生能力位/表/poll 项 + TX 门禁 + 只读 ID; 校验）。backends/ft710/ 零改动（AD-018 后果：迁移为三期，真机 A/B）。执行期设计审查抓到 3 个缺陷：①__getattr__ 无法满足 ABCMeta → 四个后端类会保持抽象、实例化直接 TypeError（改为导入时绑定委托 + 清空被冻结的抽象集合 + 守卫测试）；②模式必须是整数寄存器（RadioBackend.set_mode(mode_num: int)/RadioState.mode: int），且 FTX-1 的 H/I 不是十六进制字符 —— 原设计 f"MD0{num:X}" 会发出无效命令 MD011（改为显式 mode_codes 查表，set_mode(0x11) → MD0I）；③poll 项与 initial_state_sync 必须返回已解析的 RadioState 字段值且键名必须匹配（vfo_a_freq 而非 frequency）。频谱：Yaesu CAT 参考中不存在波形（SS 仅为设置；Hamlib 整个 Yaesu 家族无 scope 代码）→ scope_type="none"，由既有 S 表高斯回退覆盖 UI，前端无需改动。双接收（FTDX101D/MP 与 FTX-1F 的固有能力）只记录不实现，留二期。测试：新增 5 个模块 106 例（profile 不变式、传输/命令层、后端门禁与身份、pty 假电台往返、诊断助手、注册表接线），全套 962 例全绿；Hamlib 模拟器对等测试为可选（skipUnless 已构建的 simftdx101 + socat）。执行中还修掉假电台自身的两个缺陷（阻塞 os.read 线程导致事件循环关停挂死 → 改 loop.add_reader；未实现 FA/FB 写）。验证边界：无真机 → TX 默认拒绝、ID; 期望值三台未知（仅记录）、音频采样率按 44.1 kHz 假设（TODO(hw-verify)）、表头曲线列入 unverified_meters；_diag_yaesu.py 负责闭合。
SDD V2.45 2026-09-12 Claude 录音写入端生命周期修复 ——「同一进程里只有第一次录音有声音」。 现场日志（22:26:53）：Recording started 之后 6 ms 就出现第一条 Recording writer is falling behind — dropping audio，随后每 ~1.1 s 一条（每 50 块），21.9 秒累计 950+ 次丢弃，最后以 Recording queue full on stop — finishing directly 收尾；产物 15515kHz_20260912_222653.mp3 经 ffmpeg volumedetect 实测 max_volume = -91 dB（数字静音），时长 21.92 s 与真实会话一致 —— 大小和时长都正确、内容全哑。根因（代码证据 + 本机复现，现场日志签名逐行复现）：_recording_writer_loop 是每会话一次性的 —— 消费 stop 哨兵、刷盘定稿后即 return（关闭流程正是靠这个语义 await 它），但任务只在 lifespan 里 create_task 一次。于是首次 REC/STOP 之后 writer 永久消失、再无消费者：200 块（≈4 s）的有界队列在毫秒级填满，此后每一块都走 QueueFull 丢弃分支，stop() 再按时间戳把整条时间轴补静音。「第一条丢弃出现在开始后 6 ms」正是铁证：队列在会话开始前就已经是满的（上一次会话留下的残留）。旁证（与「每个进程只有第一份录音有声音」一致，期间服务重启过）：recordings/ 中 07050kHz_221811 -4.2 dB、15515kHz_222508 -3.2 dB、15800kHz_215337 -0.4 dB 有真实音频，其余 7 份均为 -91.0 dB（含分析期间旧进程在 22:30:15 又产出的一份，同样全哑）。修复：(1) 新增幂等的 _ensure_rec_writer() —— 任务已结束或为 None 即重建，由音频块入队、录音开始、stop 入队三处调用，活跃会话永远有消费者；任务因异常死亡时会 WARNING「Recording writer failed — restarting it」而不是静默丢弃（正是本次事故中最缺的可观测性）；(2) 队列满时的 stop 回退不再在事件循环上直接 session.stop()（那会与 writer 线程的 add_audio 并发写同一时间轴，违反单写者不变式），改为丢弃最旧一块为哨兵腾位，MP3 定稿仍由 writer 线程独享。回归测试 RecorderWriterLivenessTests ×3（同进程第二次 REC 必须真正收到音频；writer 异常死亡必须 WARNING 并重建；满队列时 stop 必须入队而非就地执行），修复前双双为红。测试 909（+3）。文档：AD-017（writer 生命周期与队列语义）、操作指南「丢失 N 块」判定段、AGENTS.md/tests-README 计数。边界：V2.44 的「专用单线程池」改动（14aff85）消除的是另一类风险（录音与串口/音频争抢默认 executor 导致饥饿），与本次生命周期根因无关，两者互补、均保留；真机 8 小时以上连续录制与树莓派 SD 卡长时间写入仍未验证。
SDD V2.44 2026-09-12 Claude v1.15.0 发布：macOS 已发布，Windows 当晚补发布；并修复“同进程第二次录音全静音”。 发布前抓到并修复的缺陷：MP3 writer 任务其实是每会话一次性的（收尾后返回，关停时靠 await 它），但原先只在进程启动时创建一次 —— 首次 REC/STOP 之后任务已结束，后续每次录音队列无人消费，200 块积压秒级灌满、音频全部被丢弃，stop() 再补出静音，于是得到“长度正确、内容 100% 静音”的 MP3（现场 22:26 Recording dropped 950 block(s) so far）。修复：_ensure_rec_writer() 在每次 add_audio/stop 入队/每次 REC 时幂等重建（发现 writer 已死时告警），队列满改为丢最旧块腾位给 stop 哨兵（不再在事件循环里同步 stop）；新增 3 个 writer 存活测试（该步共 909；随后启动器修复 +10，发布产物由 919 项测试把关）。关键教训：两个安装包分别在 22:03 与 22:18 构建，而该修复写于 22:34 —— 已上传的包不含修复；同时我最初用 strings 搜索打包产物里的函数名来判断“包是否含修复”是无效方法（PyInstaller 的 PYZ 是压缩的），改用 PyInstaller.archive.readers.CArchiveReader 提取入口脚本并遍历代码对象后才得到可信结论（macOS 重建包确认含 _ensure_rec_writer）。macOS（已发布）：MRRC-Modern-v1.15.0-arm64.dmg（55,976,374 bytes，SHA-256 d66cb2adff34a5f0a4d85c64d20d97d448eed7eb278a58acffdb4852aa55099e）—— 修复后重建，包内 lameenc.cpython-313-darwin.so、Contents/Frameworks 符号链接、前端 v29 与录音面板均已核验，lame.js 已移除。Windows 已发布（补记，同日稍晚）：原 v1.15.0 构建（45,491,710 bytes，构建于修复前）在发布窗口内被主动撤下（网站上 MRRC-Modern-Setup.exe 回退至 v1.14.2 归档），未对外公告；ham.vlsc.net（Win11 KVM 宿主）恢复后从同一 commit 重建为 MRRC-Modern-v1.15.0-Windows-x64-Setup.exe（45,494,587 bytes，SHA-256 SDD V2.43 2026-09-12 Claude CAT 掉线恢复成本修复 + 日志静音（现场：USB 串口桥反复重枚举）。 现场日志证据：[Errno 6] Device not configured（macOS ENXIO）+ 紧随其后 No such file or directory: /dev/cu.usbserial-0121DB3A0，且当时的可用端口列表里没有电台的桥 → 不是读超时误判，而是 USB 串口桥物理层消失后重新枚举（当日累计 171 次，分布突发：9/09 112 次、9/11 6 次）。真正的性能问题在恢复路径：watchdog 重连后调用 initial_state_sync() —— FT-710 的 24 条串口查询（FA/FB/VS/MD0/TX/SM0/SH0/AG/PC/PA0/RA0/NB0/NR0/BC/AC/PS/SS01/AN/GT/CO/MS/AO/RI0/RG0，每条 0.25 s 超时 + 20 ms 间隔），实测占住串口锁 17:10:23→17:11:25 ≈ 62 秒（日志在这段时间完全空白），期间用户命令排队、且同步会 mark 全部字段 dirty（连带把 2026-08-15 加的 serial_connected 诊断刷了 84 行）。修复：重连不再跑全量同步 —— 那 24 个字段全部由轮询分层覆盖（IF 0.1 s 频率/模式/S表、VFO 与 TX 0.5 s、设置 2 s、慢遥测 5 s），重连只需恢复 serial_connected 并照旧重跑 scope init（EX040101/EX040200），未读到的字段保留上次已知值而不再被默认值清零（原实现经 RadioState.from_sync_result + 全字段 setattr 会清零 —— 这也是不能部分同步的原因）。日志纪律：reconnect_loop 首次尝试 INFO、后续 DEBUG、掉线超过 RECONNECT_HINT_AFTER_ATTEMPTS（5 次 ≈ 15 s 退避）后只发一条 WARNING 给出物理层提示（USB 线/供电/勿用无源 HUB）；connect() 失败仅首次打印 ERROR + 可用端口列表、后续降为 DEBUG；新增 CatController._is_device_gone() 识别 ENXIO/ENODEV/ENOENT（并在读错误处只提示一次）；2026-08-15 的 state broadcast contains serial_connected 诊断降为 DEBUG。测试 906（+3）。保留的物理层边界：重枚举本身是硬件/驱动事件（电缆、供电、无源 HUB、与 FT4222 频谱共用同一条 USB），软件侧只能做到快速恢复 + 静默 + 可操作提示，已登记为 §13 R11。
SDD V2.42 2026-09-12 Claude 服务端 QSO 录音（增量 MP3）—— 替换浏览器录音器，修复回放颤抖。 根因（代码证据）：旧录音器把到达的帧顺序拼接、无时间戳，于是网络抖动与 jitter buffer 的补帧被写进文件（queue.length > 50 的丢弃与 WS 停顿都成了时间轴压缩）；采样率错配已排除（AudioRX_sampleRate 恒为 48 kHz，Opus 解码亦 48 kHz）。新架构：recorder.py 把 RX（AudioHandler.read_rx_chunk() 的设备域 PCM，44.1k/48k）与 TX（/WSaudioTX 解码出的 48 kHz 麦克风 PCM）放到单一时钟轴上（time.monotonic_ns() 定位、源切换重锚定、50 ms 连续性容差吸收调度抖动、真实停顿补静音），经 44.1k→48k（audio_resample，AD-011 唯一 SRC 桥）与 48k→16k（96 抽头 Hamming 窗 sinc FIR 抗混叠抽取，移植 mrrc）得到 16 kHz 单声道存储域，用 lameenc 增量编码边录边落盘（每块 flush → 崩溃时磁盘上仍有可播放前缀；RAM 恒定，对比 mrrc 的内存缓冲 1 小时省 ~115 MB）。并发与接线：单 writer 任务独占会话（asyncio.to_thread 编码，事件循环零阻塞），有界队列（200 块≈4 s，带丢弃计数）防内存失控；RX 循环的 idle 深睡条件改为「无监听且未在录音」，否则无人收听时 REC 录到空白；TX 侧只在 radio.tx_status == 0 期间录 RX（避免自监听重复）。控制面：复用已鉴权的 /WSradio —— set{field:"recording"} 启停（放在 “Radio not connected” 守卫之前：录音依赖 USB 音频而非 CAT，无 CAT 时文件名显示 00000kHz，与 mrrc 一致），stop 哨兵携带 completion future 以便写盘完成后再广播；recordingState 变更即广播 + 录制中 1 Hz（复用 RX 循环 20 ms tick，不新增任务）；fullState 携带同一快照 → 新客户端/刷新即同步。文件面：三条 REST 路由（列表 / Range 的 FileResponse 流（实测 206 + Content-Range，播放器可 seek）/ 删除），名称白名单 ^\d{5}kHz_\d{8}_\d{6}\.mp3$ + containment 校验（I8 教训），正在录制的那份删除返回 409。索引 recordings.json（与 mem_channels.json 同处）合并磁盘：用户自行放入的 mp3 也能显示（时长按大小/码率推算）。前端：REC 按钮改发服务端命令并渲染广播状态（含实时计时，无本地计时器）；新增「录音」面板（总占用、频率/日期/时长/大小、单实例 <audio> 播放、下载、删除，录制中禁用删除），全部 createElement/textContent（XSS 门禁）。删除浏览器录音器与 lame.js（530 KB）；cache-bust main v29 / ui v31 / sw mrrc-v32。依赖与打包：lameenc>=1.8.0（单编译扩展模块，非包）+ PyInstaller hiddenimport；两个 launcher 增加 MRRC_RECORDINGS_DIR（用户数据目录）。测试 891（+22）。硬件边界：真机 RX tap 的音频连续性、TX 实测音质、mac DMG/Win 安装包内 lameenc 的加载与录制、树莓派 SD 卡长时间录制 —— 均未验证，须在对应平台各录一段实测。文档：SDD §5 NFR-066、§7.2 实体、§8 AD-017、§9 音频链路、§10/§11 Recorder、§12 接口与运维、§13 R10/A8、AGENTS.md/README/tests-README/CHANGELOG、操作指南与树莓派指南、网站指南段落。
SDD V2.41 2026-09-12 Claude ICOM SDR model support: IC-705 / IC-7610 / IC-7760 behind a profile-driven CI-V core — with no hardware present for any of the three. The Icom side of the backend layer became data-driven: new backends/ic7300/civ_profiles.py holds one CivModelProfile per model (CI-V address, 1a 05 <item> Transceive set-mode item, scope geometry, bands, mode registers, attenuator steps, meter curves, verification flags, data provenance) and civ_codec/civ_controller/civ_scope take those values as optional parameters whose defaults reproduce the previous IC-7300 constants exactly — the three new backends are ~10-line subclasses overriding _profile, backends/__init__.py gained known_models() (the server’s model whitelist and the per-model baud table are now registry-derived, closing the class of bug where a registered backend stays unreachable), and no server.py model branches were added. New radios: IC-705 (0xA4, 475-bin scope, HF+6m+2m/70cm, 10 W), IC-7610 (0x98, 689-bin/15-segment scope, 0-45 dB attenuator in 3 dB steps, dual receiver) and IC-7760 (0xB2, same scope geometry, 200 W). All profiles carry verified=False, list their unverified_meters, and refuse PTT/TUNE until the operator sets MRRC_ALLOW_UNVERIFIED_TX=1 — an unverified radio can receive, but cannot key; a release is never gated (Chapter 15 layering), and the server answers a refused key-up with actionable text instead of silence. RadioCapabilities gained verified/tx_gated/dual_rx/unverified_meters/audio_gain_boost, RadioState gained the dirty-tracked model_mismatch, and the model-ID check (19 00, read-only) logs the observed bytes without inventing a verdict — no profile records an expected ID, because a fabricated expectation would raise false mismatch warnings on working radios. New _diag_civ.py drives the production CivController through identity → read-only probes → scope measurement (which yields the three facts that cannot be settled offline: bin count, amplitude ceiling, segment count) → static meters including raw Vd/Id → an optional 200 ms TX check with PTT read-back and manual recovery steps, then restores state and writes a paste-ready Markdown report naming civ_profiles.py as the correction target. Bug found while implementing the ATT step list: the attenuator data byte is packed BCD, not decimal (the plan’s range(0,46,3) would have sent 0x0F for 15 dB); new encode_bcd_byte/decode_bcd_byte helpers carry the IC-7300MK2 CI-V reference evidence, and the IC-7300’s 20 dB byte stays bit-identical at 0x20. Frontend: connection dialog lists the three models with a 实验性 label, RX playback boost comes from capabilities instead of a model-string comparison, and a badge + one-time notice explains a disabled transmitter (cache-bust main v28 / ui v30 / sw mrrc-v31). Packaging: backends.ic7300.civ_profiles added to the PyInstaller hiddenimports. Suite 820 tests across 43 modules (was 724/39). Hardware boundary: no unit of any of the three models was available, so the 689-bin geometry, the three models’ meter curves (inherited from the IC-7300 and rescaled by rated power), the 48 kHz USB-audio assumption and every 19 00 model ID remain unverified and are listed as assumption A7 / risk R9 in §13; _diag_civ.py is how a field report turns them into facts. Docs: SDD §5 (NFR-032/041/060/065), §8 AD-016 amendment, §9, §11, §13 (A7/R9), §15 (new outermost gate layer), AGENTS.md module table + env vars, README supported-radio table, tests/README counts, IC-7300_硬件验收清单.md pointer.

SDD V2.39 2026-09-10 Claude IC-7300 S-meter flicker fix — scope “no data” sentinel (hotfix folded into the v1.14.2 installers, rebuilt same day). Field report: the IC-7300’s web S-meter flickered/jumped constantly. Root cause: the IC-7300’s CI-V scope segments carry NO S-meter byte (unlike the FT-710’s FT4222 frame at data[110]), so ScopeHandler.s_meter stayed at its 0-initialized value — a legal S-zero — and _on_scope_frame’s >= 0 gate broadcast it on every ~30 fps waveform, forcing radio.s_meter back to 0 between the 10 Hz CAT poll (15 02) updates that wrote the true reading: the UI flipped between the two continuously. Fix: the “no data” sentinel is now -1, skipped by the existing >= 0 gate; FT-710 per-frame real values and the synthetic fallback (update_from_radio_state seeds the true radio value) are unchanged. Also fixes the pre-existing Optional[callable] type misuse in ScopeHandler (builtins.callable is not a type). 3 new sentinel tests; suite 724 tests. Docs: SDD README Quick Facts, CHANGELOG v1.14.2. Real-radio flicker acceptance on the field rig remains an operator check. Same-day rebuild: the macOS DMG was rebuilt with this fix and redeployed (55,898,038 bytes, SHA-256 88ed7c626e86b8cb2ba03a5ce2419c9313fece0e97eb579da75b8dda6c6bbb75, replacing the V2.38 build); the Windows installer was rebuilt the same night after the jump-host outage cleared (45,435,022 bytes, SHA-256 a7ee16674c0db29a80fbb072301581a3ecf4805e6705382db380ea534862c706, 724 tests on the VM) and redeployed alongside the DMG.

SDD V2.37 2026-09-10 Claude IC-7300 model↔︎baud linkage + audio duplicate recovery (field log 2026-09-10: CI-V scope stalled → S-meter fallback, and RX/TX audio -9999 on every PTT). Root cause one: the legacy installer templates pre-fill MRRC_BAUD_RATE=38400 (the FT-710 value) regardless of model; that stale env value overrode the backend-aware default (115200, V2.24) and starved the IC-7300 CI-V 0x27 scope stream (stall watchdog: no complete waveform for >3s). Root cause two: the connection dialog’s saved device name (“麦克风 (USB Audio CODEC)”, no trailing space) matched ONLY the WDM-KS duplicate of the codec — the MME/WASAPI entries carry an invisible trailing space before “)” — and WDM-KS opens fail with -9999 on the field rig, while all 6 retries re-resolved the same locked index. Fixes: (1) api_setup_save now writes MRRC_BAUD_RATE=default_baud_for(radio_model) (new config.default_baud_for() helper) on every save and logs the radio-menu expectation (CI-V baud = Auto or match); (2) apply_first_run aligns the stored baud with the probed model when unset or still at the legacy template value (explicit custom values preserved); (3) windows/default.env + macos/default.env leave the baud empty (per-model default; both templates’ stale 38400 removed). Audio: _find_rx/_tx_device gain an exclude tier filter — configured-name matches now collect ALL host-API duplicates, prefer a non-WDM-KS entry (same hardware opens fine via MME/DirectSound/WASAPI) and log the alternates; the post-_reinit_pyaudio retry in start_rx/start_tx re-resolves with the failed index excluded, falling through to the codec-hints tier so a WDM-KS-locked name lands on the working MME duplicate (keeps the original index when no other candidate exists — preserves the V2.12 USB-re-enumeration recovery). Device-selection logs now include the host API. pyaudio ImportError fallback is an empty module stub (HAS_PYAUDIO guards unchanged) instead of None so optional-dependency environments keep valid type structure. 28 new tests (config default_baud_for ×4; first-run baud linkage ×4; api_setup_save linkage ×3; host-API preference/exclude tiers ×4; end-to-end WDM-KS→MME recovery for RX and TX ×2; remainder in existing classes); suite 721 tests across 39 modules. Docs: SDD README Quick Facts, AGENTS.md test counts, tests/README counts, CHANGELOG (unreleased). Real-radio acceptance (IC-7300 scope at 115200 with the CI-V baud menu on Auto; audio recovery on the field rig) remains an operator check.

SDD V2.32 2026-09-07 Kimi RX broadcast no longer drops catch-up frames. _audio_rx_loop previously trimmed bursts to the newest AUDIO_RX_MAX_FRAMES_PER_CYCLE = 2 Opus frames whenever the loop fell behind and read_rx_chunk() returned up to 4 chunks at once — the discarded frames were permanent losses, leaving holes in the client jitter buffer (audible flutter) and in browser recordings. The trim is removed: every encoded frame is sent. The burst stays bounded by the read-side cap (min(count, chunk*4) ≈ 80 ms), and the client’s time-based jitter buffer absorbs it. Complements the V2.31 restart_rx() mitigation for the muffled-frame recording issue (the trim produced gaps, not the low-frequency/attenuated frames themselves). Suite 693 tests green.
SDD V2.31 2026-09-06 OpenAI RX recording flutter mitigation. Field analysis of the 2026-09-06 mrrc-qso-* browser recordings showed intermittent 20 ms RX frames whose energy was low-frequency/attenuated, while TX mic frames were clean. The RX read/encode/send code was unchanged since the 2026-08-23 baseline, and offline checks ruled out browser Opus decode, lamejs MP3 encode, chunked 44.1→48 kHz resampling, packet drop, and synthetic event-loop stalls as standalone causes. The remaining field-consistent failure mode is a long-lived PortAudio RX capture stream degraded after TX playback on the same USB codec, previously documented only for Windows. AudioHandler.restart_rx() now reopens the RX capture stream after every TX→RX transition on all platforms, still off the asyncio loop; this preserves the codec/device sample-rate contract and extends the existing full-duplex recovery guard to macOS/CoreAudio-like stacks. Regression tests updated for platform-independent restart behavior.

SDD V2.13 2026-07-28 Kimi Header power switch WITHDRAWN before release — FT-710 CAT power control is too fragile for a remote UI. V2.11 added the button, V2.12 hardened it; day-2 guarded live testing delivered the verdict: with the radio warm, CAT healthy, and all guards active, a single PS0→PS1 cycle failed — 3 verified PS1 attempts over 36 s never woke the radio (PS1 attempt 1/2/3: radio not answering in the server log), and it again needed physical intervention. Combined with the V2.12 MCU-wedge incident, the feature cannot meet the reliability bar for remote operation, so per the user’s standing instruction the UI button is removed (header vfo-indicator restored). Kept: (1) the power WS command for the maintenance scripts (_power_cycle*.py), now with the V2.12 guards (15 s boot window, PS1 retry≤3× + FA read-back verify, PS0 double-send, TX-active refusal) so the script path cannot wedge the MCU either; (2) Tier-3 PS; poll for truthful power_on state; (3) the PortAudio re-init fix — that one is independently load-bearing (every power cycle re-enumerates the sound card). Also learned: the 36 s synchronous verify loop inside the WS handler starves that connection’s keepalive (server closed the client with 1011 ping-timeout) — acceptable on the maintenance path, would have needed a background task for UI use. test_power_switch.py stays as the guard’s regression coverage; suite 435 tests. SDD V2.12 2026-07-27 Kimi Power-command hardening + PortAudio re-enumeration survival (live power-cycle test fallout). Reliability testing of the V2.11 header power switch on the real radio produced two field failures. (1) Boot-interrupted PS0 wedges the CAT MCU: a rapid PS0→PS1→PS0 sequence (PS0 landing seconds after PS1, mid-boot) left the radio with serial/audio/scope USB all enumerated but CAT permanently deaf — direct serial probes (FA;/ID;/PS;, both CP2105 channels, all DTR/RTS combos) answered nothing; only a physical power cycle recovers. A timing probe showed the healthy envelope: PS1 20 s after PS0 boots the radio in ~4 s. Guards added to the power set handler: 15 s boot window after PS1 during which PS0 is rejected with a UI error; PS1 retried ≤3× and verified by a real FA; query before power_on flips (a lost fire-and-forget PS1 previously left the radio silently off); PS0 sent twice; power-off refused while transmitting. (2) Audio dies after any radio power cycle: the FT-710’s whole USB hub (sound card included) re-enumerates on power-off/on, invalidating the CoreAudio device IDs cached inside PortAudio at Pa_Initialize time — every TX/RX stream open then failed -9999 paUnanticipatedHostError until a server restart (the “TX audio device unavailable” field report). audio_handler._reinit_pyaudio() (terminate + re-init) is now invoked once on TX/RX open failure, with device index re-resolved by name before retrying; index-locked FT710_AUDIO_RX/TX_DEVICE=<n> configs remain fragile across re-enumeration — name locking recommended. Diagnosis note: py-spy on the wedged server proved the poll loops were alive and querying (all timing out) — the “stall” was radio-side, not a scheduler deadlock. 12 new tests (test_power_switch.py 9 + PortAudioReinitTests 3); suite 435 tests. Docs: §10.4 power row, tests/README counts + module list, CHANGELOG v1.7.7.

SDD V2.10 2026-07-26 Kimi HTTPS by default on Windows — self-signed bootstrap (minimal approach). Root cause of “HTTPS can’t be enabled on a fresh install”: windows/launcher.py hardcoded --no-ssl in build_command(), and server.py only enables SSL when cert+key already exist — a fresh install has neither (the Win11 KVM rig only got HTTPS via a bypass ps1 that ran ft710-server.exe directly with FT710_SSL_CERT/KEY). Per user direction this is the minimal fix, not a local-CA/trust-chain design: new ssl_bootstrap.py generates a throwaway self-signed cert on first run (ECDSA P-256, 10-year, SANs = localhost / hostname / hostname.local / 127.0.0.1 / ::1 / detected LAN IPs, idempotent reuse, graceful None when cryptography missing); launcher ssl_material() honours explicit FT710_SSL_CERT/FT710_SSL_KEY first, FT710_SSL=off restores HTTP; build_command(ssl_pair) passes --ssl-cert/--ssl-key instead of the hardcoded --no-ssl; local_url(secure=) picks the scheme; wait_for_server probes with an unverified context (self-signed isn’t trusted by any store). Why HTTPS matters off-localhost: plain HTTP on a LAN address is not a secure context — browsers disable AudioWorklet and getUserMedia (the exact failure seen during remote phone testing). cryptography>=41 uncommented in requirements.txt (PyInstaller bundles it via the launcher spec’s repo-root pathex). server.py unchanged (existing --ssl-cert/--ssl-key + file-exists logic). 12 new tests (test_ssl_bootstrap.py + launcher SSL tests); suite 421 tests. Docs: AGENTS.md module table, tests/README counts, CHANGELOG v1.7.6, WINDOWS_INSTALLER_GUIDE HTTPS note, win_pack.md §2.4 sidecar scripts marked superseded.

SDD V2.9 2026-07-26 Kimi Windows TX crackle fix — WASAPI 48 kHz output (Win11 KVM field report: transmitted audio “咔啦咔啦” noise instead of voice). Measured on the rig: the C-Media codec’s MME 44.1 kHz playback path paces ~1.4× slow (50×20 ms stream.write() block 1.36–1.42 s instead of 1.00 s at 44.1/48/32/16/8 kHz; only 22050 Hz paces 1.02), so the TX drain loop falls behind, the 400 ms queue cap drops 24–34 % of voice frames (TX session written/fed 330/432, 195/296 — macOS production shows 96–97 %), chopping transmitted audio into crackle. The codec’s WASAPI entry at its native 48 kHz mix rate paces correctly (0.96). Fix: start_tx() prefers the same-name WASAPI entry on Windows (_wasapi_tx_variant) and opens the stream at that entry’s native rate; feed_tx_audio passes 48 kHz PCM straight through at 48 kHz (no 48→44.1 resample) and pre-buffer/cap/graceful-drain budgets are computed from the actual stream rate (getattr fallbacks keep module constants for 44.1 kHz). macOS unchanged (CoreAudio device domain stays 44.1 kHz); RX capture stays MME 44.1 kHz on Windows (paces fine). v1.7.5 hotfix: the v1.7.4 branch referenced sys.platform without the module import (NameError on every PTT) — new end-to-end StartTxWindowsTests cover start_tx on both platforms. AD-011 amended: device-domain rate is host-API-dependent, not universally 44.1 kHz — this overrides the audio-pyaudio-rate warn rule with runtime evidence. 7 new tests (WindowsWasapiTxTests + StartTxWindowsTests); suite 409 tests. Docs: AD-011, AGENTS.md module table, tests/README counts, CHANGELOG v1.7.4/v1.7.5.
——— —— ——– ———
SDD V2.8 2026-07-26 Kimi Windows RX audio dies after first PTT (full-duplex wedge) + scope resync that actually works (Win11 KVM field report: audio perfect after server restart, gone after one PTT). RX root cause: on Windows (MME/DirectSound), opening the TX playback stream on the FT-710’s C-Media USB codec silently wedges the RX capture stream — it stays open and error-free but delivers silence; macOS CoreAudio is unaffected (hardware capture verified healthy, max 43% FS, with the server stopped). Fix: AudioHandler.restart_rx() reopens the capture stream on every TX→RX transition, hooked in _broadcast_state on tx_status dirty (covers PTT/TUNE/physical PTT/dead-man), Windows-only no-op elsewhere (macOS must not pay the reopen cost per PTT). Scope root cause: the byte-by-byte sync_stream resync (wfview port) could never succeed on the FT4222 — every 1-byte SingleRead is its own SPI transaction (CS toggles per call), making a contiguous multi-byte sync pattern unobservable; it only consumed the stream and churned recovery into fatal:too_many_reinits after every PTT (V2.7’s TX-pause resume path used it). Replaced with resync_device() (close → 1 s idle-bus settle → reopen — the pattern that empirically realigns, same as a pipe restart), used by both the TX→RX resume and the bad-frame path; MAX_SYNC_ATTEMPTS/sync_attempts_this_device removed. AD-008 amended (Windows full-duplex wedge + restart_rx). 4 new tests (RestartRxTests); suite 402 tests. Docs: AD-008, AGENTS.md module table, tests/README counts, CHANGELOG v1.7.3.
——— —— ——– ———
SDD V2.7 2026-07-26 Kimi TX-safe spectrum: scope_pipe TX pause + Windows process-tree kill (field log from the Win11 KVM test rig: after every PTT the waterfall showed fallback/garbage for 30–45 s). Root cause: the FT-710 garbles its scope stream during TX (normal radio behavior), but scope_pipe was TX-agnostic — it kept reading, tripped sync_lost → spi_stalled device re-init → more sync failures → fatal:too_many_reinits, then a server-side pipe restart before real FFT frames returned. Second defect surfaced by the same log: on Windows, killing the PyInstaller onefile bootloader (proc.terminate()) orphaned the real worker, which kept the FT4222 open, so the next pipe failed FT_OpenEx with FT_DEVICE_NOT_FOUND for ~10–15 s (matches the long-standing code comment). Fixes: (1) the pipe protocol gains a stdin control channel — the server pushes TX:1/TX:0 on every tx_status dirty broadcast (_notify_scope_pipe_tx, covers PTT/TUNE from any source, re-synced with force=True on pipe start); while TX is active the pipe pauses SPI reads and freezes all sync/stall/reinit counters (tx_pause STATUS), and on TX→RX runs one clean sync_stream re-sync (tx_resume:resync); (2) Windows pipe teardown uses taskkill /PID <pid> /T /F (_terminate_process_tree_sync, POSIX keeps SIGTERM), and the pipe treats stdin EOF (parent died) as a shutdown signal — a second orphan guard alongside the stdout-heartbeat EPIPE path. Frontend: waterfall/FFT canvases show “TX 发射中 — 频谱暂停” while tx_status != 0 instead of stale rows. AD-005 amended (stdin control channel + tree kill), §9.5.1 diagram updated, UC-004 flow unchanged from the client’s perspective. 13 new tests (test_scope_pipe_tx.py: control-line parsing, TX-notify transitions/force/dead-pipe guard, taskkill vs SIGTERM); suite 398 tests. Docs: AD-005, §9.5.1, AGENTS.md module table, tests/README counts, CHANGELOG v1.7.2.

SDD V2.6 2026-07-26 Kimi Windows RX/TX audio device lock + setup guide. Field report: Windows install with working CAT/FT4222 spectrum had broken audio — the FT-710’s built-in USB sound card enumerates on Windows under a generic name (no “FT-710”/“YAESU” in it): USB Audio CODEC on some driver/OS builds, USB Audio Device on others (confirmed on the reporter’s machine), possibly localized like 麦克风 (USB Audio Device) or prefixed 2- USB Audio CODEC. AD-008 name matching missed it and the mono/full-duplex heuristics grabbed a laptop mic (RX) or PC speakers (TX). audio_handler.py adds a generic USB-audio name tier (USB_AUDIO_NAME_HINTS, case-insensitive substring) after the FT-710-specific names and before the channel heuristics, for both _find_rx_device and _find_tx_device; multiple matches (per-host-API duplicates open the same hardware) use the first and log a warning pointing at the env-var lock. windows/default.env now pre-locks FT710_AUDIO_RX_DEVICE/FT710_AUDIO_TX_DEVICE=USB Audio — the common substring of both enumeration forms (name is reboot-stable; index is not). docs/WINDOWS_INSTALLER_GUIDE.md gains an “Audio (RX/TX) Setup” section: device identification from the startup PortAudio device list, env-var locking, Windows sound-panel guidance (never make the radio’s USB audio the default playback device — system sounds would modulate the transmitter; optional 16-bit/44.1kHz formats), and the radio-side modulation routing verified against the FT-710 Operation Manual — RADIO SETTING → MODE SSB (and AM/FM/PSK-DATA) → MOD SOURCE = USB (default AUTO selects by transmission method), RPTT SELECT stays OFF since PTT is CAT-keyed. Troubleshooting table gains 4 audio rows. SDD: AD-008 decision/rationale, NFR-065, §12.2 config table, I2 marked Resolved (implemented under the FT710_AUDIO_RX/TX_DEVICE names). 8 new tests (USBCodecDeviceSelectionTests); suite 385 tests. Docs: tests/README counts, AGENTS.md module table, README env examples.

SDD V2.4 2026-07-25 Kimi Optional ATR1000 networked antenna-tuner linkage, ported from the sibling mrrc project. Default disabled: FT710_ATR1000_HOST (empty = off) + FT710_ATR1000_PORT (default 60001); when off there is no client task, no network traffic, linkage hooks short-circuit, /WSatr1000 closes with code 4000, and the frontend module stays inert — zero impact on existing installs. New backend modules: atr1000_client.py (asyncio-native WS client to the tuner hardware: binary frames [0xFF,CMD,LEN,DATA], 5s reconnect, 55-min proactive refresh, TX-no-SYNC policy with 15s watchdog, tuning-clear heuristics, 5s-throttled relay writes, LearningBuffer 4-sample stability-window learning, notify_freq/notify_tx sync hooks, on_change callback) and atr1000_tuner.py (TunerStorage LC-learning JSON store ported verbatim: learn gate SWR 1.0–1.8, 1kHz keys ±5kHz nearest, atomic writes; store file atr1000_tuner.json). 3 linkage behaviors: (1) freq change → auto-apply learned LC relays (hooked in _broadcast_state on vfo_a_freq/vfo_b_freq/active_vfo dirty — dirty holds source fields only); (2) TX on/off → notify_tx (device push mode, learning window); (3) server-side tune assist _atr_tune_assist(): TX2 carrier → skip if SWR≤1.6 → snapshot relays → full tune (mode=2) → keep+learn if SWR improved ≥0.02 else rollback relays → carrier always dropped in finally (module constants ATR_TUNE_*: 0.3s settle, 5s min, 45s deadline, 0.8s compare settle, 2.5s meter wait). New 5th WS endpoint /WSatr1000 (?token= auth, JSON both ways): server→client atrState (connected/power/swr/sw/ind/cap/ind_uh/cap_pf/tuning/tx/freq/last_update), atrTuneResult (start/skipped/success/rollback/error), pong; client→server atrTune, ping. fullState gains atr1000Enabled. Radio-internal TUNE (TX2+AC003) untouched. Architecture simplification vs mrrc: its 4-layer chain (browser→Tornado→unix socket→proxy→device) collapsed to a single-process direct async client; its browser-side tune assist moved server-side (avoids mrrc’s documented PTT-delay/main-thread-blocking incident); ATR data deliberately kept out of RadioState (separate channel, same precedent as spectrum); audio path untouched. Cache busting: sw.js ft710-v21, css v18, main v21, ui v20, settings_manager v13, atr1000.js v1. 98 new tests across 3 files (test_atr1000_tuner.py 36, test_atr1000_client.py 50, test_atr1000_server.py 12); suite 372 tests across 21 modules. Docs: AGENTS.md module table + env vars, Ch9 §9.8, Ch10 service row, Ch11 component rows, Ch15 tune-assist note, tests/README counts, README WS/env tables. Follow-up (2026-07-26): lifespan primes the linkage with the current freq/TX at boot (startup state sync predates the client); frozen-app store path — FT710_ATR1000_STORE env overrides the TunerStorage file (Windows launcher points it at the user data dir so learned data stays writable and survives reinstalls), PyInstaller spec hiddenimports both ATR modules; startup log line for the enabled feature. P0 — packaged web UI 404: PyInstaller 6 onedir places datas under _internal/, but STATIC_DIR resolved next to the exe, so the installed app served only the inline fallback login page and “Static files not found” (the V1.6.0 “web login OK” verification passed because the fallback page works). server.py gains _resource_dir() (sys._MEIPASS-aware, same pattern scope_libraries.get_resource_roots() already used) for STATIC_DIR; launcher’s starter-channel seeding also checks _internal/mem_channels.json. P1 — scope_pipe orphan: on Windows, proc.terminate() kills only the onefile bootloader; the real child inherited the stdout pipe, blocked forever on write, and held the FT4222 device. scope_pipe.py now emits the len=0 stdout heartbeat its docstring always promised (1 s) — a dead parent closes the pipe, the next write raises EPIPE, and the pipe exits through its normal cleanup. P1 — silent build failures: build.ps1 wrapped all native commands (python, pyinstaller, iscc) in Invoke-Checked ($LASTEXITCODE — $ErrorActionPreference never covered native commands), and no longer ships windows/__pycache__. P2 — launcher self-spawn chain: frozen launcher with a missing ft710-server.exe (e.g. antivirus quarantine) used to “fall back” to [sys.executable, server.py] = spawning itself recursively; it now reports the missing exe and exits 1. P2 — version drift: installer AppVersion 1.6.0 → 1.6.3 (app CHANGELOG v1.6.3); new packaging/windows/requirements-build.txt pins pyinstaller==6.21.0; installer guide updated. SDD V2.1 2026-07-22 Kimi “PTT keys but no voice / no RF power” soft failure fixed (long-uptime classic). Root cause: /WSaudioTX uplink ownership was assigned only at connect time — with multiple clients (browser tab + iOS app, or a zombie socket surviving a network flap), an idle first-connected client kept ownership while the PTT-ing client’s mic frames were silently dropped (ws is not _tx_owner_ws → continue); and once the owner disconnected, every remaining client stayed muted forever (no promotion). Log evidence: repeated Audio TX client connected (2-3 total, owner=False) clusters with frantic TX audio started retries, RM5=0/1 during keyed sessions. Fixes in server.py: (1) _promote_tx_owner() hands the uplink to a remaining client on owner disconnect; (2) _claim_tx_owner_for_token() — on PTT key-up the keying client (matched by auth token, now tracked per socket) claims ownership; (3) non-owner drops are counted and logged rate-limited. TX-path observability: per-session counters (frames received / decoded / decode-fail / non-owner drops in server; written / write_err / PCM peak via AudioHandler.tx_stats()) logged at every PTT release as TX session: … — the next silent failure will identify its own broken link from the log alone; first write error per session is now WARNING-level (was debug). Also reviewed: iOS TUNE button now runs the real tune sequence (TX2+AC003, press-and-hold), iOS RX AVAudioPlayerNode ScheduleBuffer channel-mismatch crash fixed (live output format + mono upmix), iOS header S-value dedup, _poll_if TypeError + serial_connected recovery (V2.0, same day). 5 new tests (TXUplinkOwnershipTests); suite 262 tests. Docs: Ch15 Layer 4 ownership note, tests/README counts.

SDD V2.0 2026-07-21 Kimi Spurious “电台未连接” (radio disconnected) fixed. Live log (2026-07-21 22:48): every _poll_if cycle raised TypeError: unsupported operand type(s) for -: 'int' and 'NoneType' in the freq-change logging guard (abs(freq - _last_logged_freq) with _last_logged_freq=None, introduced 2026-07-17). The exception fired before state.update(), so every poll counted as a failure; after 5 consecutive failures serial_connected=False was broadcast and the waterfall showed “电台未连接 — 检查 USB / 电源” while the radio was in fact fully operational (user commands/band changes all OK). Two fixes in poll_scheduler.py: (1) None-guard the first-poll logging condition; (2) recovery path — a successful poll now flips serial_connected back to True (previously only the watchdog’s full reconnect did, so even a genuine transient timeout streak stuck the flag until reconnect). 1 new regression test (IFPollRecoveryTests); suite 257 tests. Docs: tests/README counts.
SDD V1.9 2026-07-21 Kimi Spectrum freeze after USB reconnect fixed. Live log (2026-07-21 22:18): the serial device briefly disappeared (USB re-enumeration), CAT auto-reconnected, but the spectrum never recovered — scope_pipe logged spi_stalled:reinit_1/5 and then silence. Root cause: the connection watchdog’s reconnect path re-synced radio state but never re-ran the scope-init CAT sequence (EX040101/EX040200); a USB re-enumeration resets the FT-710’s scope output, so the radio simply stops sending FFT frames and the FT4222-side SPI re-init cannot help. The waterfall then sits frozen on stale data while serial_connected=true, so the V1.8 “radio disconnected” hint never shows. Fix: PollScheduler gains an on_reconnected async hook invoked by _connection_watchdog after a successful reconnect + state re-sync; server.py wires it to _init_scope_cat. Hook failures are logged and non-fatal. 2 new regression tests (WatchdogReconnectTests); suite 252 tests. Docs: §9.6 Tier-5 line, Ch11 PollScheduler row, tests/README counts.

SDD V1.7 2026-07-19 Kimi SSB filter switching race fixed (“works sometimes, ignores other clicks”). Root cause: _poll_settings checked skip_next_poll only BEFORE each Tier-3 query; a SH0; response already in flight when the user’s filter command arrived carried the pre-command width and was applied unconditionally at the end of the 13-query cycle (~0.5 s later), snapping the web UI back to the stale filter until the post-skip poll corrected it ~4 s on. Whether a click landed inside that in-flight window decided “works / doesn’t work” — the radio itself applied every SH00<NN>; correctly (live log: all sets ok=True, read-backs match). Fixes: (1) settings poll re-checks _should_skip(field) + _polling_paused() AFTER each query await and discards stale reads — same guard pattern _poll_if/_poll_vfo already had, now covering all 13 Tier-3 fields (filter, gains, preamp, att, NR, NB, AN, tuner, scope, antenna, AGC, meter display); (2) the filter/filter_width WS handler waits 150 ms after SH00<NN>;, reads SH0; back, and broadcasts the radio’s ACTUAL index (logged as Filter read-back: index=N (requested M)) so a silently-rejected index would self-correct in ~0.2 s instead of ~4 s — live verification showed N==M for all SSB indexes 9/13/17/20/23, confirming the width table. 3 new regression tests (test_settings_poll_discards_stale_reads_after_query, test_filter_set_reads_back_actual_width, plus the pre-existing skip-before-set ordering assertion); suite now 223 tests, all passing. Docs: §9.6, AD-009, §10.4 filter row, README polling note, AGENTS.md module table, tests/README counts.

RX audio: removed blocking stderr diagnostic prints (30 fps flush), parallelized WS audio sends via asyncio.gather. CAT robustness: AI1 disabled (interfered with FA/MD0/SM0), AI0 sent before ID query. restart.sh: fixed .env source. Updated gap-analysis doc (DN correction).
SDD V1.1 2026-07-06 Claude Bug fix: TX audio chain unified to 48 kHz (was 16 kHz mic → 48 kHz playback = crackling). Enhanced _find_rx_device() with multi-layer detection (config env var, name match, mono-channel heuristic, full-duplex TX). Enhanced _find_tx_device() with same heuristics. opus_rx.py: TX_RATE 16k→48k, added TX_FRAME_SAMPLES constant. tx_opus_worker.js: FRAME_SIZE 320→960, OpusEncoder 16k→48k. ft710_main.js: getUserMedia + AudioContext sampleRate 16k→48k (both main + fallback). TX Opus encoder optimized for voice: CBR 28kbps, VBR/FEC/DTX/HPF disabled. Added SSL CLI args. Event-based power button (mobile-first connect). Added AD-011 (Unified 48kHz TX audio pipeline). AudioContext gain explicitly set to 1.0. iOS unlock oscillator during user gesture.
SDD V1.0 2026-07-06 Claude Initial SDD baseline for MRRC FT-710 codebase. All 15 chapters written from scratch based on current repository state: server.py, cat_controller.py, audio_handler.py, opus_rx.py, radio_state.py, poll_scheduler.py, scope_handler.py, scope_pipe.py, config.py, static/ (HTML, CSS, JS, modules, worklets, worker). Documents: Full CAT control, FT4222 scope + S-meter fallback, Opus/PCM dual-codec bidirectional audio, AudioWorklet RX playback, TX mic capture, 5-tier polling, dirty-field state broadcasting, session auth, memory channels, PTT safety architecture.

v1.24.4 — 2026-10-02 — 一个 hub、一个地址、入口就是呼号

  • 门户收敛为 https://portal.mrrc.vlsc.net/（根路径）；配置里写着 :8899、www.vlsc.net/mrrc_portal 或带 /mrrc_portal 的旧写法自动改道（:8899 实测从未对公网开放，这正是”申请成功但状态 刷不出来”的根因 —— 申请走了边缘兜底，其余请求直连 8899 全部失败）。
  • 实例入口不再带 :9988：与其他服务同在 443。
  • 入口标签 = 裸呼号（bg9aaa 而非 bg9aaa-mrrc-modern）；原因见上。
  • 启动前清理本实例残留的 frpc（实测：被杀的应用会留下 frpc 占着同名代理，下一次注册不上）。
  • 接入成功后的回执/状态带 cert_reload_required，界面据此提示并给出重启入口 —— TLS 上下文是 进程启动时建好的，新签的证书不重启不会被采用（实测：入口会一直 502）。

Design Baseline Notes

  • Radios: Eleven registered models, all superheterodyne transceivers (not SDRs), selected by MRRC_RADIO_MODEL: Yaesu FT-710 (verified, default); Icom IC-7300 / IC-7300MK2 (verified); Icom IC-705 / IC-7610 / IC-7760 and Yaesu FTDX10 / FTDX101D / FTDX101MP / FTX-1F / FT-891 (profiles written from offline rig data, never checked against hardware — transmit is refused until MRRC_ALLOW_UNVERIFIED_TX=1, AD-019). The registry in backends/__init__.py is the validation source of truth.
  • Radio Protocols: Direct serial CAT (FT-710) or CI-V (IC-7300/MK2) via pyserial — no Hamlib, no rigctld, no TCI.
  • Scope: FT-710 uses the FTDI FT4222 SPI chip for 850-point FFT data; the Icom family uses CI-V 0x27 frames on the same serial port. The Yaesu ASCII-CAT family (FTDX10 / FTDX101D / FTDX101MP / FTX-1F / FT-891) has no scope protocol at all, so it only ever shows the synthetic spectrum. Every backend falls back to the synthetic S-meter Gaussian spectrum when real data is unavailable.
  • Audio: Bidirectional through the selected radio’s built-in USB audio interface via PyAudio. FT-710: 44.1kHz native device rate; IC-7300/MK2: 48kHz native. Opus codec (RX 64kbps, TX 64kbps CBR, both 48kHz) with Int16 PCM fallback. audio_resample.py bridges the 48kHz codec domain ↔︎ 44.1kHz device domain for the FT-710 only (960↔︎882 frame-aligned).
  • Auth: Shared-password session auth, single role split into operator / listen-only (MRRC_LISTEN_PASSWORD). Cookie mrrc_auth (30-day); transport is header → cookie → deprecated ?token= (AD-024 — no frontend builds a token URL any more, and the query form exists only so already-installed native clients keep working until they ship the header path).
  • PTT Safety: Client-side PTT watchdog (500ms) + dead-man switch (WS disconnect) + pagehide handler + server-side TX status poll (0.5s). No TX0 verify loop on release (radio obeys TX0 fire-and-forget). TX audio drain=50ms. TUNE is press-and-hold.
  • Polling: 7-task adaptive scheduler: IF 0.1s (FA/MD0/SM0), VFO 0.5s (VS/FB), TX status 0.5s, TX meters 0.5s (RM3/4/5/6, TX only), settings 2s, slow 5s, connection watchdog 1s. Poll queries use 0.25s timeout and cooperate with priority-command preemption (_cancel_polls). Poll loops re-check skip state AFTER each in-flight query response and discard stale reads; filter sets are verified by a 150 ms SH0; read-back. After a successful watchdog reconnect the scheduler re-runs the scope-init CAT sequence (EX040101/EX040200) via its on_reconnected hook — a USB re-enumeration resets the radio’s scope output.
  • Meters: RM3-8 raw 0-255 values calibrated via piecewise-linear tables from FT-710.rig (Power/SWR/Vd/Id). Frontend displays watts, SWR ratio, volts, amps — not raw numbers.

Key Architecture Decisions

ID Topic
AD-001 FastAPI/Uvicorn backend
AD-002 Direct serial CAT (no Hamlib)
AD-003 Dirty-field state broadcasting
AD-004 Tagged dual-codec audio (Opus + PCM)
AD-005 scope_pipe standalone subprocess
AD-006 Dual-mode spectrum (FT4222 + fallback)
AD-007 PTT release safety flow (simplified: no verify loop, 50ms drain)
AD-008 PyAudio FT-710 auto-detection
AD-009 7-tier adaptive polling with bounded lock occupancy
AD-010 Memory channels as server-side JSON
AD-011 48 kHz codec / per-backend device-rate bridge
AD-012 Active-VFO tracking (VS poll + FB poll — target active VFO on freq set)
AD-013 Meter calibration tables (piecewise-linear from FT-710.rig)
AD-014 AI mode disabled — active polling; DN=step-down not DNR
AD-015 Priority CAT command preemption (_cancel_polls + send_priority_set_command)
AD-016 Pluggable radio backend architecture (FT-710 + IC-7300 via RadioBackend/Capabilities)
AD-017 Server-side QSO recording (incremental MP3, 16 kHz storage domain)
AD-018 Profile-driven Yaesu ASCII-CAT core (FTDX10/FTDX101D/MP/FTX-1F/FT-891); the verified FT-710 path stays separate
AD-019 Unverified models ship receive-only (transmit gate + read-only identity check + per-table provenance)
AD-020 One-touch CQ (server-side one-shot automated transmission reusing the PTT safety chain)
AD-021 Support diagnostics bundle (server-built + allow-list redaction + dedicated receiver)
AD-022 Upgrade channel (generated manifest + self-proving state + transmit gate)
AD-023 Remote-session telemetry (listener concurrency + uplink, counts without identifiers)
AD-024 Session token transport (cookie first, header for native clients, query deprecated)
AD-025 Spectrum bandwidth tiers (shape × divider, gated by spectrumCaps negotiation)
AD-026 Setup hotspot and the password-less onboarding window (opened by network path, not a flag)
AD-027 Factory-preset (ship-ready) box image: --preflash, off by default; fixed credentials + system half baked in; Wi-Fi and the public entry configured from /settings
AD-028 Local control surface: Bluetooth HID remote → evdev → /WSradio (local_ctl.py, mrrc-localctl); HID-usage keymap; PTT release on every path; HandlePowerKey=ignore
AD-029 Independent SDR receiver (public web receiver): sdr_pipe.py owns the device, sdr_dsp.c is the two-stage channelizer, sdr_hub.py arbitrates the single tuner; own password role, 8 demodulators, 50 listeners
AD-030 Serial instrument (NanoVNA-H): nano/vna.py owns the port, scan_bin binary frames validated by exact length, nano/analysis.py derives SWR/Z/resonance; four guards and the device sweep restored after every scan; upgrade via ROM DFU, locked by MRRC_NANO_ALLOW_FLASH
